# Function oracle cases for the engineering, database and remaining math functions. Lines starting with @ put a value in a
# cell of the one sheet: a number, "text" in quotes, TRUE or FALSE, an error such as #N/A, or =formula. Every other line is a
# formula, evaluated in column L, one row each; tools/excel-function-oracle.ps1 runs them through Excel and writes
# functions-engineering.excel.tsv, which FunctionOracleTests checks Egap against. Decimal commas in text belong to the PC's
# locale, nb-NO, in which Excel ran. Inputs live in columns A to J.
